# Railway Control Center, Er-Natvir (2931): the wonder window. Rewritten on 11 November 2026 by
# "Claude files/tools/er_natvir_2026-11-11/gen.py"; hand-owned since. The .gui side is the window
# "DA_Railway_HQ_Window" in interface/topbar.gui. ROOT and FROM are the country that looks at the window.
#
# The upgrade button restores the hall (tiers I to III): I lights the board and opens the workshop,
# II frees the signal desks (overload, shutdown), III opens the survey desks (new lines, second upgrades).
# The board has one lamp for each of the 31 roads: 13 sections of the old Dwarovrod and 18 lines that can be
# laid. A click on a lamp sets the country flag DA_RW_sel_<n>; the desk buttons of that road show.
# Every desk button needs every province of its road (DA_RW_owned_<n>).
# Triggers: common/scripted_triggers/DA_Railway_triggers.txt. Effects: common/scripted_effects/DA_Railway_effects.txt.
# Story: events/DA_Hold_Er_Natvir.txt. The reports of the workshop: events/DA_Railway_events.txt.

custom_window = {
	name = DA_Railway_HQ_Window
	potential = {
		DA_has_Racial_Interface_on_Dwarven_wonders_open = yes
		has_country_flag = DA_show_wonder_Railway_HQ
	}
}

custom_button = {
	name = DA_close_button_Railway_HQ

	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	trigger = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		clr_country_flag = DA_show_wonder_Railway_HQ
	}
}

# BEGIN DA_WW level-up button (build_wonder_pages.py)
# The works take time: the tier and what it brings arrive when DA_WW_finish_2931 runs
# (common/scripted_effects/DA_Wonder_Works_effects.txt). Materials are taken here, ducats by DA_WW_begin_2931.
custom_button = {
	name = DA_Railway_HQ_upgrade_button
	tooltip = DA_RW_upgrade_button_tt
	potential = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = DA_WW_building_2931 }
		NOT = { DA_WW_maxed_2931 = yes }
	}
	trigger = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_Wonder_in_use_tt
			NOT = { has_country_flag = DA_Wonder_DA_Railway_HQ_in_use }
		}
		2931 = {
			owned_by = prev
			has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 0 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 1 } } } }
			2931 = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = DA_tooltip_has_dig_level_4_or_higher
					DA_has_dig_level_4_or_higher = yes
				}
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_total_required = { amount = 10 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_total_required = { amount = 10 }
			DA_arcane_metal_total_required = { amount = 10 }
			DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 1 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 2 } } } }
			2931 = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = DA_tooltip_has_dig_level_6_or_higher
					DA_has_dig_level_6_or_higher = yes
				}
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_total_required = { amount = 20 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_total_required = { amount = 20 }
			DA_arcane_metal_total_required = { amount = 20 }
			DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 2 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 3 } } } }
			2931 = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = DA_tooltip_has_dig_level_8_or_higher
					DA_has_dig_level_8_or_higher = yes
				}
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_total_required = { amount = 40 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_total_required = { amount = 40 }
			DA_arcane_metal_total_required = { amount = 40 }
			DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes
		}
	}
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = { DA_WW_refresh_2931 = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 0 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 1 } } }
				DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes    # at the price of this very day
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_spend = { amount = 10 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_spend = { amount = 10 }
			DA_arcane_metal_spend = { amount = 10 }
			DA_WW_begin_2931 = yes
			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_tier_1_unlock_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 1 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 2 } } }
				DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes    # at the price of this very day
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_spend = { amount = 20 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_spend = { amount = 20 }
			DA_arcane_metal_spend = { amount = 20 }
			DA_WW_begin_2931 = yes
			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_tier_2_unlock_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				2931 = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 2 } NOT = { has_great_project = { type = DA_Railway_HQ tier = 3 } } }
				DA_WW_can_pay_2931 = yes    # at the price of this very day
			}
			DA_alchemy_reagents_spend = { amount = 40 }
			DA_enchanted_building_material_spend = { amount = 40 }
			DA_arcane_metal_spend = { amount = 40 }
			DA_WW_begin_2931 = yes
			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_tier_3_unlock_tt
		}
	}
}
# END DA_WW

custom_icon = { # frames: 1 the dark board, 2 tier I, 3 tier II, 4 tier III with the surveyed lines (make_railway_art.py)
	name = DA_RW_board
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	frame = { number = 4 trigger = { DA_RW_tier_3 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 3 trigger = { DA_RW_tier_2 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 2 trigger = { DA_RW_tier_1 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { always = yes } }
}

# lamps: frame 1 dark (the board is dark, or the road is not wholly ours), 2 ruined, 3 restored, 4 advanced,
# 5 shut down, 6 overloaded, 7 a line not laid, 8 a line being laid

	name = DA_RW_lamp_1
	tooltip = DA_RW_lamp_1_tt
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_tier_1 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_owned_1 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 5 trigger = { DA_RW_shut_1 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 6 trigger = { DA_RW_over_1 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 2 trigger = { DA_RW_ruined_1 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 3 trigger = { DA_RW_restored_1 = yes } }

		always = yes
	}

		hidden_effect = {

			set_country_flag = DA_RW_sel_1

# ---- 1 The Western Dwarovrod
custom_button = { # railway works in every province that lacks a developed upgrade
	name = DA_RW_upgrade_1
	tooltip = DA_RW_upgrade_tt
	potential = {
		has_country_flag = DA_RW_sel_1
	}
	frame = { number = 4 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_tier_1 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { always = yes } }
	trigger = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_needs_tier_1_tt
			DA_RW_tier_1 = yes
		}
		DA_RW_owned_1 = yes
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_upgrade_needed_tt
			DA_RW_can_upgrade_1 = yes
		}
		treasury = 270
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = DA_RW_upgrade_started_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			2864 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						DA_upgradable_railway_province = yes
						NOT = { has_construction = any }
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						building = DA_railway_work
						speed = 1
						cost = 1
					}
				}
			}
			2935 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						DA_upgradable_railway_province = yes
						NOT = { has_construction = any }
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						building = DA_railway_work
						speed = 1
						cost = 1
					}
				}
			}
			2865 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						DA_upgradable_railway_province = yes
						NOT = { has_construction = any }
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						building = DA_railway_work
						speed = 1
						cost = 1
					}
				}
			}
			2866 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						DA_upgradable_railway_province = yes
						NOT = { has_construction = any }

			hidden_effect = {

				990 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }

custom_button = { # two years of overload
	name = DA_RW_overload_19
	tooltip = DA_RW_overload_tt
	potential = {
		has_country_flag = DA_RW_sel_19
	}
	frame = { number = 2 trigger = { DA_RW_over_19 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 4 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_tier_2 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { always = yes } }
	trigger = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_needs_tier_2_tt
			DA_RW_tier_2 = yes
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_line_laid_tt
			DA_RW_laid_19 = yes
		}
		DA_RW_owned_19 = yes
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_not_shut_tt
			NOT = { DA_RW_shut_19 = yes }
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_not_overloaded_tt
			NOT = { DA_RW_over_19 = yes }
		}
		adm_power = 50
	}
	effect = {
		adm_power_cost = 50
		custom_tooltip = DA_RW_overload_applied_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			849 = { add_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_overload duration = 730 } }
			845 = { add_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_overload duration = 730 } }
			843 = { add_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_overload duration = 730 } }
			833 = { add_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_overload duration = 730 } }
			832 = { add_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_overload duration = 730 } }
		}
	}
}
custom_button = { # shut the road, or open it again
	name = DA_RW_shutdown_19
	tooltip = DA_RW_shutdown_tt
	potential = {
		has_country_flag = DA_RW_sel_19
	}
	frame = { number = 2 trigger = { DA_RW_shut_19 = yes } }
	frame = { number = 4 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_tier_2 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { always = yes } }
	trigger = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_needs_tier_2_tt
			DA_RW_tier_2 = yes
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_line_laid_tt
			DA_RW_laid_19 = yes
		}
		DA_RW_owned_19 = yes
		adm_power = 50
	}
	effect = {
		adm_power_cost = 50
		if = {
			limit = { DA_RW_shut_19 = yes }
			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_shutdown_revoked_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				849 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }
				845 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }
				843 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }
				833 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }
				832 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_shutdown_applied_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				849 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				845 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				843 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				833 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				832 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			DA_RW_recount = yes
		}
	}
}

				4462 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }

				4459 = { remove_province_modifier = DA_railway_mod_shut_down }

			}
		}

			custom_tooltip = DA_RW_shutdown_applied_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				4462 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				4463 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }
				4459 = { add_permanent_province_modifier = { name = DA_railway_mod_shut_down duration = -1 } }

			}
		}

			DA_RW_recount = yes
		}
	}
}

custom_button = { # Improved Railway I: frame 3 once we have it, 2 while the workshop is at it, 4 while it cannot be started
	name = DA_RW_research_faster1
	tooltip = DA_RW_research_faster1_tt
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	frame = { number = 3 trigger = { has_country_flag = DA_rail_faster1_c } }
	frame = { number = 2 trigger = { has_country_flag = DA_RW_res_faster1 } }
	frame = { number = 4 trigger = { NOT = { DA_RW_tier_1 = yes } } }
	frame = { number = 1 trigger = { always = yes } }
	trigger = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_needs_tier_1_tt
			DA_RW_tier_1 = yes
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_not_known_tt
			NOT = { has_country_flag = DA_rail_faster1_c }
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_RW_workshop_free_tt
			NOT = { has_country_modifier = DA_researching_rails }
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_has_3_ADK
			check_variable = {
				which = ancientDwarvenKnowledge
				value = 3
			}
		}
		treasury = 500
	}
	effect = {
		add_treasury = -500
		custom_tooltip = DA_Cost_3_ADK
		add_country_modifier = {
			name = DA_researching_rails
			duration = 730
		}
		custom_tooltip = DA_RW_research_started_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_variable = { ancientDwarvenKnowledge = 3 }
			set_country_flag = DA_RW_res_faster1
			country_event = { id = DA_railway_event.5 days = 730 }
		}
	}
}

		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = DA_has_6_ADK
			check_variable = {
				which = ancientDwarvenKnowledge
				value = 6
			}
		}
		treasury = 1500
	}
	effect = {
		add_treasury = -1500
		custom_tooltip = DA_Cost_6_ADK
		add_country_modifier = {
			name = DA_researching_rails
			duration = 1095
		}
		custom_tooltip = DA_RW_research_started_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_variable = { ancientDwarvenKnowledge = 6 }
			set_country_flag = DA_RW_res_trade2
			country_event = { id = DA_railway_event.10 days = 1095 }
		}
	}
}

	name = DA_RW_research_trade2_text

		always = yes
	}

}
